About:

### **LeafSheep — AI-Managed Liquidity for Cetus DLMM on Sui**

**Earn trading fees. Execute your market thesis. Put idle assets to work. All on the same capital.**

### **What It Does**

LeafSheep wraps a Cetus DLMM position inside an on-chain **Position Manager (PM)** and delegates management to an AI agent. The agent continuously places liquidity at the right price bins so that users:

**Earn fees and capture alpha simultaneously.** Every time the market crosses a filled bin, you collect the swap fee *and* execute the trade embedded in your bin shape — buy low on a dip, sell high on a rally, on the same capital.

**Keep idle assets earning.** When part of the position drifts outside the active price range, those coins are automatically routed into Scallop or Kai lending — instead of sitting idle on-chain.

**Bring your own agent, pay zero protocol fees.** LeafSheep's built-in managed service charges 20% on collected earnings. Authorise your own agent and that fee is waived — the contract locks the protocol worker out, and all earnings stay in your PM.

### **What Was Built**

**Move contract** — A Position Manager object model with a three-tier permission system (Owner / Agent / Protocol). Key invariant: Agent and Protocol tiers are mutually exclusive at the contract level — authorising an agent locks out the protocol worker and simultaneously waives the 20% managed-service fee. `DEP_ONLY` upgrade policy means protocol logic is permanently frozen post-deploy; only dependency version pointers (Cetus DLMM, Scallop, Kai) can be updated.

**Four TypeScript SDKs** — `cdpm-sdk-user`, `cdpm-sdk-agent`, `cdpm-sdk-protocol`, `cdpm-sdk-bin-math` — covering the full interaction surface for end users, agent developers, protocol operators, and bin-distribution calculations for all 7 strategy shapes.

**Strategy worker** — A TypeScript/Bun background process that polls active bins, evaluates each PM against its strategy family, builds Programmable Transaction Blocks, and submits via gRPC. Lending supply/redeem (Scallop sCoin, Kai YT) is inlined into the same PTB as the rebalance — no separate transaction.

**Frontend** — React SPA deployed on Cloudflare Pages. All transactions signed by the user's wallet; chain state read directly via Sui GraphQL — no API backend.

### **Protocol Design**

**Permission model — three tiers, no escalation path:**

TierRebalanceWithdrawProtocol feeOwner (user's wallet)✓✓0%Agent (authorised AI)✓✗0%Protocol (only when no agent)✓✗20%

**Open agent market.** Any address can be authorised as an agent — the contract enforces *what* agents can do, not *who* can be one. Third-party AI developers can deploy strategies and compete on live performance without protocol permission.

**Lending integration.** Scallop and Kai supply/redeem are inlined into the same PTB as the rebalance. Protocol fee applies to interest only, not principal.

### **7 Strategies**

BID-ASK, ONE-SIDE BID, ONE-SIDE ASK, ONLY-BID, ONLY-ASK, CURVE, SPOT — each maps a distinct market thesis to a bin distribution shape, with defined rotation conditions.

Full visual playbook: [*https://app.leafsheep.xyz/playbook*](https://app.leafsheep.xyz/playbook)

### **Technical Details**

**Contract — Live on Sui Mainnet**

`0x573584cc4698e82fd85f2b54e64ad4cd901c42b768f7628ec167bf2d24aa2aa7
`Upgrade policy: `UpgradePolicy::DEP_ONLY` — bytecode permanently frozen; only dependency package pointers can be updated.

**Security**

- Formal verification: 5 specs proven (fee cap ≤ 50%, lending conservation, accumulation monotonicity)

- AI-audited: 3 findings fixed — see `SECURITY_AUDIT.md`

About:

SuiX Protocol is live on Sui mainnet. We're building the index fund-style exposure layer for the Sui ecosystem; making it easier for both new and advanced users to participate without the complexity of traditional DeFi.

Pre-Existing Work

Our SuiX and SuiX5 index vaults and analytics infrastructure were live before Overflow 2026. We're disclosing these as pre-existing work. During Overflow we built zkLogin onboarding for the vaults product, and built SuiX Utility from the ground up.

What We Built During Overflow

zkLogin Onboarding (Vaults)

We integrated Sui's zkLogin SDK with our own self-hosted prover for the vaults product, so new users can sign in with Google and get a wallet instantly; no seed phrase, no browser extension. Shown in the demo video.

SuiX Utility

The idea is simple; your wallet is your portfolio. Connect your wallet and choose from curated indexes ( SuiX5, SuiX10, SuiX Meme, SuiX DeFi, and SuiX Stack ) each recomputed every 12 hours from live market data. The platform shows you your current allocation, your target, and how far off you are. When you're ready, you rebalance in one transaction using PTBs routed through Cetus. Your assets never leave your wallet.

Users have three options for how they want to manage things:

State 1 — Manual. Check drift yourself and rebalance when you want.

State 2 — Telegram notifications. Get alerted automatically when your portfolio drifts past your threshold. Credentials are encrypted with Seal so we store zero PII.

State 3 — Full automation. Create a dedicated automation wallet, encrypt the private key with Seal's on-chain policy system, and let SuiX handle everything. We only decrypt when drift conditions are met on-chain. No keys stored. No credentials stored. Completely hands-free.

Move Contracts on Mainnet

Two contracts deployed during the hackathon window.

Automation Package

0x65436b396702ba21d3c5cc0849aa0d83e7bff7d4fc90d22088d64f74aef73e5e

Notification Package

0xc09469d5816468c49d136d6f47ceb43e86560789457816652d431c76c7460ee5

Integrations

zkLogin · Seal · SuiNS · Cetus · Sui

Vision

SuiX Protocol is building the onboarding and portfolio management layer for the Sui ecosystem. The goal is to make Sui the home for the next generation of index fund-style investing; non-custodial, automated, and accessible to anyone.

About:

SuiPump is a permissionless bonding-curve token launchpad on Sui. Anyone can launch a fungible token in 2 wallet signatures, no coding required. Price discovery happens automatically through an on-chain constant-product AMM, and tokens graduate to one of three DEXes — Cetus, Turbos, or DeepBook — once they reach the graduation threshold.

Why SuiPump is different:

- Oracle-based dynamic graduation — the graduation threshold is recomputed on every buy against SUI's live USD price (Pyth), so every token graduates at a consistent dollar market cap regardless of where SUI trades. We haven't seen another Sui launchpad do this.

- 1% trade fee with 40% going directly to creators — the highest direct creator share on Sui, undiluted by staking-token middle layers.

- 10% LP fee retained in the curve, deepening liquidity over time.

- Front-run-safe graduation — all fund routing is automatic and internal.

- Transferable CreatorCap with up to 10 payout recipients.

- Season 1 airdrop — 50% of protocol fees distributed to early users.

Current state: Live on Sui testnet, stress-tested to 658,000+ SUI in trading volume. 55/55 Move unit tests + 59/59 Python harness passing. Internal pre-audit security review complete with zero critical, high, or medium-severity findings. Targeting mainnet after an independent third-party Move audit.

Tech stack: Move smart contracts (fully on-chain, non-custodial — no funds leave the protocol's control) + React/Vite frontend with @mysten/dapp-kit, plus a Talus Nexus autonomous trading agent with Rust signed-HTTP tool servers. A lightweight off-chain indexer provides read-only data and real-time feeds; it holds no funds and sits in no transaction path.

Target: $50M+/month trading volume within 12 months of mainnet launch.

About:

TIDE is a Sui-native operating layer for BTC treasuries. It helps holders and treasury operators define policy-driven rules for liquidity, drawdowns, debt, buffers, and repayment behavior before capital moves.

Instead of treating BTC treasury management as a manual spreadsheet or a black-box bot, TIDE turns each decision into a reviewable and verifiable workflow: model the position, stress-test BTC price paths, choose a rail profile, review the policy, and produce an on-chain receipt that explains what was decided and what evidence supported it.

The long-term vision is a non-custodial BTCfi cockpit where operators keep control of their wallets while TIDE provides policy logic, simulation, proof bundles, rail-aware readbacks, and auditable execution history across Sui-based lending and liquidity venues.

TIDE uses Sui Move for policy and receipt objects, Walrus for proof bundle storage, and oracle/readback inputs such as Pyth and protocol state to make treasury decisions transparent, reproducible, and inspectable.
